Method: 借鉴Gabriel Tarde和James Mark Baldwin的模仿理论，构建多层级框架，在词汇、语义、概念、结构和叙事五个层面，使用方向性对齐（directional alignment）和受控校准的相似度度量（control calibrated similarity measures）对文学文本进行比较，生成"转化轮廓"（transformation profiles）。

Result: 将模型应用于历史上有记录的文学关系中，发现不同的作品对在不同表示层级上保留源文本结构的程度各异，同时在其它层级上表现出分歧，从而证实了选择性转化的存在并可被量化。

Conclusion: 转化轮廓为文学研究中提供了一种定量方法，能够系统刻画模仿如何在特定层面持续存在以及创造性分歧在何处发生，从而将创造力重新概念化为多层级选择性转化的产物。

Abstract: Creativity is often framed as the production of novelty, yet many cultural works emerge through transformation of earlier artifacts and not through isolated invention. Drawing on theories of imitation by Gabriel Tarde and James Mark Baldwin, this paper models creativity as selective transformation across multiple levels of textual representation. We introduce a multi-level framework that compares literary texts across lexical, semantic, conceptual, structural, and narrative dimensions using directional alignment and control calibrated similarity measures. Applying the model to historically documented literary relationships, we show that different pairs preserve source structure at different representational levels while diverging in others. These transformation profiles provide a quantitative method for characterizing how imitation persists and where creative divergence occurs within literary works.

</details>


### [167] [AI systems and the reproduction of (standard) language ideologies in World Englishes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28528)
*Kingsley Ugwuanyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI如何反映、强化并偶尔挑战英语语言意识形态，指出AI系统在训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共话语等层面再现了标准英语霸权，并通过"delve"一词争议等案例说明全球北方英语使用者对全球南方英语使用者的语言规范监控，同时提出了"标准化悖论"——AI既可能 homogenize 英语也可能通过多元语料实现英语的多元化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速增长复活了社会语言学与世界英语研究中的经典问题：谁来决定什么是合法的英语，谁的英语是可疑的。作者希望揭示AI系统及其使用如何在各个层面再现并强化标准语言意识形态，从而呼吁更包容的AI设计方法。

Method: 论文采用多源证据的质性分析方法，综合了实证研究、媒体评论、社交媒体辩论以及AI输出实例，以公共争议（尤其是对'delve'一词的过度关注）为案例，从训练数据、设计协议、评估基准、用户反馈和公共评论五个层面分析AI中的语言意识形态再生产机制，并引入Christian Mair提出的"标准化悖论"作为理论框架。

Result: 研究表明AI系统在多个操作层面系统性地再现了核心圈英语规范并边缘化非主流英语变体；全球北方英语使用者的语言规范监控行为通过AI话语得以延续；AI同时存在标准化与多元化的悖论张力，非主流英语变体在公共讨论中越来越被误认为AI生成的语言。

Conclusion: 论文主张将AI系统视为语言意识形态（重新）生产的场所，呼吁采取更具包容性的设计方法，承认英语的多元性，以应对将某些英语视为比其他英语更合法所带来的现实负面后果。生成式AI正在世界英语研究的既有框架下重新点燃关于标准化、合法性与英语所有权等长期争论。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language models (LLMs) has resurrected age-old questions in sociolinguistics and world Englishes, such as who decides what counts as legitimate English, whose English is suspect etc. This paper examines how AI systems, their uses and discourse on them reflect, reinforce, and occasionally challenge (standard) language ideologies, which privilege Inner Circle norms and marginalize non-dominant Englishes. Drawing on evidence from empirical studies, media commentary, social media debates, and examples from AI outputs, the paper shows that AI technologies reproduce dominant language ideologies at different levels: training data, design protocols, evaluation benchmarks, user feedback and public commentary. The analysis uses the public controversy over AI-sounding language, especially the fixation on the word delve, to illustrate how speakers of English from the Global North police the English language norms of Global South English users. The paper also identifies what Christian Mair has called a "standardisation paradox": AI may homogenize English by privileging standard forms and at the same time pluralize Englishes through exposure to wide-ranging corpora and annotation work carried out by Global South users. In doing so, the paper argues that generative AI is reigniting long-standing debates in World Englishes about standardization, legitimacy, and the ownership of English, now playing out in algorithmic systems, model training, evaluation practices, and public discourse, where non-dominant Englishes are increasingly conflated with AI-generated speech. Discussing AI systems as a site where language ideologies are (re)produced, the paper argues for more inclusive design approaches that recognize the plurality of Englishes in order to address the real-world negative consequences of treating some as more legitimate than others.

</details>


### [168] [ORCA-bench: How Ready Are Language Model Agents for Oncall?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28545)
*Albert Gong,Kyuseong Choi,Abhineet Agarwal,Jason Schechner,Ryan Huang,Raj Agrawal,Anish Agarwal,Raaz Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ORCA-bench是一个将通用编码智能体置于生产级oncall场景的基准测试，包含一个OpenTelemetry插桩的微服务系统（六天指标、日志、追踪数据），配以1,079个RCA任务。在五个前沿智能体中，最佳RCA准确率在中等难度任务上仅为25.3%，在困难任务上为10.0%，最弱模型在40%的报告中产生幻觉性根因，证明前沿编码智能体距离可靠承担生产可靠性职责仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在代码编写、补丁和搜索方面能力突出，但oncall根因分析（RCA）需要在模糊的用户报告中，对噪声指标、日志、追踪和源代码进行推理，这与常规编码任务本质不同。缺乏一个生产保真度的基准来衡量通用编码智能体在这类任务上的实际表现，因此作者构建了ORCA-bench来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建一个OpenTelemetry插桩的实时微服务测试系统，通过真实遥测接口（Prometheus、Jaeger、Grafana中的OpenSearch）暴露6天50GB的指标、日志和追踪数据，并提供完整源代码访问。在此基础上设计1,079个RCA任务，系统性地变化报告特异性、检测时间延迟和共现故障场景。金标准症状由SRE专家策划并签署，LLM作为评审者经人工二次评分（Cohen's κ_w=0.90），评估五个前沿agent的RCA能力。

Result: 最佳模型在Medium难度（现实输入设置）上RCA准确率为25.3%，在Hard难度上仅10.0%；即使用Claude Fable 5仍存在差距。最弱模型在40%的事故报告中产生幻觉性不可信的根因。移除源代码访问导致所有指标下降。

Conclusion: 即便在条件相对理想的公共测试环境（50GB/六天、代码和插桩公开、任务隔离调查）中，前沿编码智能体的RCA表现仍然很低。真实生产系统规模更大、动态性更强、更加独特，因此报告的差距是前沿编码智能体被安全地赋予生产可靠性职责所需工程投入的下界。

Abstract: Large language models can write, patch, and search code, but oncall root cause analysis (RCA) demands something different: reasoning over noisy metrics, logs, traces, and source code, starting from ambiguous user-facing reports, often hours after the incident began. We introduce ORCA-bench, a benchmark that puts general-purpose coding agents in a production-fidelity oncall setting. ORCA-bench pairs a live OpenTelemetry-instrumented microservice system--exposing six days of metrics, logs, and traces through real telemetry interfaces (Prometheus, Jaeger, and OpenSearch via Grafana) and full source-code access--with 1,079 RCA tasks that systematically vary report specificity, time-to-detection, and co-occurring fault scenarios. Ground-truth symptoms are curated and signed off by expert SREs, and our LLM-as-judge is independently re-scored by humans (Cohen's $κ_w=0.90$). Across five frontier agents, the best RCA Accuracy is 25.3% on Medium-difficulty tasks (the realistic-input setting) and 10.0% on Hard--a gap that remains even with Claude Fable 5. The weakest model hallucinates an implausible root cause in 40% of incident reports, and removing source-code access degrades every metric. Crucially, these are performances on a curated 50 GB / six-day testbed with tasks investigated in isolation on a system whose code and instrumentation are public. Since real production systems are order of magnitudes larger, more dynamic, and more idiosyncratic, the gap we report is a lower bound on the engineering investment required before frontier coding agents can be safely entrusted with production reliability. We release the public set at https://hub.harborframework.com/datasets/orca-bench/ORCA-bench.

</details>


### [169] [Frontis-MA1: Training an AI4AI Model towards Recursive Self-Improvement in Machine Learning Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28568)
*Junlin Yang,Che Jiang,Yu Fu,Tianwei Luo,Can Ren,Weizhi Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Hongyi Liu,Yuxin Zuo,Yuru Wang,Yuchen Fan,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Xiaojian Lin,Li Sheng,Rushi Qiang,Guoli Jia,Xingtai Lv,Ermo Hua,Dianqiao Lei,Youbang Sun,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenMLE，一个用于递归自我改进（RSI）研究的开源全栈系统，包含可验证任务环境（OpenMLE-Gym）、操作符学习（OpenMLE-RL）和长时程搜索（OpenMLE-Evo）。在此基础上后训练了Frontis-MA1（35B）作为机器学习工程（MLE）的元进化智能体，围绕四个原子程序进化操作符（Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover）对齐训练和推理。在MLE-Bench Lite上，Frontis-MA1将奖牌平均值从39.39%提升至60.61%，结合OpenMLE-Evo-Max达到71.21%，超越GPT-5.5+Codex，接近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T Kimi K3；在NatureBench Lite上也展示了模型和框架的可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）需要AI系统能够改进构建AI的过程（即AI4AI），而机器学习工程（MLE）提供了一个具体、可执行的测试平台来研究这一能力。本文旨在构建一个开放的、可复现的全栈系统，用于在MLE领域系统性地研究RSI。

Method: （1）构建OpenMLE全栈系统，包括OpenMLE-Gym（可验证任务环境与执行反馈）、OpenMLE-RL（操作符学习）和OpenMLE-Evo（长时程搜索）三个组件；（2）基于该平台后训练Frontis-MA1（35B）作为MLE的元进化智能体；（3）围绕四个原子程序进化操作符（Draft、Improve、Debug、Crossover）对齐后训练与推理；（4）使用执行驱动的SFT和RL训练这些操作符，数据经过对所有评估基准的去重处理；（5）将学习的操作符合成到长时程搜索中，在学习与进化之间建立单一闭环。

Result: 在MLE-Bench Lite上（单块RTX 4090、12GB显存上限、12小时任务预算）：使用OpenMLE-Evo，Frontis-MA1将奖牌平均值从基线的39.39%提升到60.61%；使用OpenMLE-Evo-Max（引入基准无关的经验先验和异步搜索）达到71.21%，超越GPT-5.5+Codex，接近GPT-5.6 Sol和2.8T Kimi K3。在保留的NatureBench Lite上，模型与框架均具有可迁移性：固定框架时，换用训练后模型使Match-SOTA从50%提升到70%；固定模型时，换用OpenMLE-Evo使Match-SOTA从20%提升到50%。

Conclusion: OpenMLE证明了将学习与进化耦合在单一循环中能够有效推动可执行AI4AI的研究，Frontis-MA1在MLE基准上取得了显著性能提升并展现了良好的跨基准迁移能力。作者开源了模型权重和完整OpenMLE框架，以支持可复现的RSI研究方向。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires AI systems that improve the process of building AI (i.e., AI4AI); machine learning engineering (MLE) offers a concrete, executable testbed for studying this capability. We introduce OpenMLE, an open full-stack system for RSI research in MLE, spanning verifiable task environments with execution feedback (OpenMLE-Gym), operator learning (OpenMLE-RL), and long-horizon search (OpenMLE-Evo). On this stack we post-train Frontis-MA1 (35B) as a meta-evolution agent for MLE, aligning post-training and inference around four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover): the same operators are trained via execution-grounded SFT and RL on data deduplicated against all evaluation benchmarks, then composed into long-horizon search, coupling learning and evolution in a single loop. On MLE-Bench Lite under a 12-hour per-task budget on one RTX 4090 capped at 12 GB VRAM, Frontis-MA1 (35B) improves Medal Average from 39.39% to 60.61% over its base model with OpenMLE-Evo, and reaches 71.21% with OpenMLE-Evo-Max (benchmark-independent experience priors and asynchronous search), exceeding GPT-5.5 + Codex and approaching GPT-5.6 Sol and the 2.8T Kimi K3. On held-out NatureBench Lite, both components transfer: with the framework fixed, swapping in the trained model raises Match-SOTA from 50% to 70%; with the model fixed, swapping in OpenMLE-Evo raises it from 20% to 50%. We release the model weights and the full OpenMLE stack to enable reproducible research on executable AI4AI toward RSI. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/OpenRSI

</details>


### [170] [Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)
*Iliya Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过设计实验重新评估了多种语言模型自我改进方法（自我反思、批评、辩论、Best-of-N等）在相同推理预算下的表现，发现没有任何方法可靠地优于简单的重复采样并取多数票（majority voting）基线。所有36项对比中，没有一项显示方法本身优于均等成本下的重复采样，而有10项显著更差，全部涉及模型检查自身输出的方法。此外，模型规模增长时自我检查类方法的劣势缩小但未消失，而最佳N（Best-of-N）的多数投票仍优于让模型自行选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型自我提升方法（如自我反思、批评、辩论等）通过生成更多文本来提升准确率，但其收益是否真正来自方法本身的机制而非额外生成的token数量，尚不明确。Wang等人(2024)的基线比较仅提供了点估计，缺乏置信区间和显著性检验，因此需要更严格的实验设计来验证这些方法的实际价值。

Method: 采用设计实验方法，比较7种方法在1.5B、3B、7B三种规模的开源模型上的表现，使用两个数学基准测试，各150题。精确统计所有生成的token（包括批评、反思、辩论和检查的开销），将每种方法在相同推理成本下与重复采样基线进行配对比较，使用bootstrap置信区间和多重比较校正进行统计推断。

Result: 所有36项比较中没有任何方法在等成本下可靠优于重复采样；10项显著更差，全部是模型检查自身输出的方法；所有18项自我检查类比较均为负结果。Best-of-N的多数投票比让模型自己选择在1.5B规模下高8.0和11.3分，但在7B规模下仅高2.0和1.3分（不再显著）。Self-Refine和强制Reflexion在7B规模下仍比基线低3.6到10.1分。Reflexion在最小模型上从未触发重试，静默退化为单次思维链。

Conclusion: 模型自我检查类方法在相同推理预算下无法超越简单的重复采样基线；这些方法的收益主要来自额外生成的token而非方法的核心机制。自选（self-selection）的优势随模型规模增大而减弱，重复写作（rewriting）即使在更大模型上也无益。研究还发现某些方法（如Reflexion）在小模型上可能静默失效。作者发布了全部代码、提示词、生成结果和验证脚本以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Methods that make a language model plan, criticise and rewrite its own answer, reflect on mistakes, pick the best of several attempts, or debate with copies of itself nearly all make it generate far more text than a single chain of thought. Because generating more text raises accuracy by itself, a gain over one chain of thought does not show the method's idea is what helped. Wang et al. (2024) reported that a simple baseline, sampling the same question repeatedly and keeping the most common answer, often wins once budgets are comparable, but gave point estimates with no confidence intervals or significance tests.
  We rerun that comparison as a designed experiment: seven methods, open models of 1.5B, 3B and 7B parameters, two mathematics benchmarks, 150 questions each. We count every generated token, including those spent on critiques, reflections, debate turns and checking, and compare each method against repeated sampling at its own measured cost. All 36 comparisons are paired by question, with bootstrap intervals and multiplicity correction.
  No method is reliably better than repeated sampling at equal cost anywhere. Ten are reliably worse, all of them methods where the model inspects its own output, and all 18 self-inspection comparisons are negative. The two kinds of self-inspection part company as models grow. Choosing stops hurting: taking Best-of-N's eight samples and just counting the most common answer beats letting the model pick by 8.0 and 11.3 points at 1.5B, but only 2.0 and 1.3 at 7B, no longer distinguishable from zero. Rewriting does not recover: Self-Refine and a forced Reflexion stay 3.6 to 10.1 points below baseline at 7B.
  Reflexion as published never triggered its own retry on the smallest model. It judged itself correct every time and silently became a single chain of thought. We release code, prompts, all generations, and our verification scripts.

</details>


### [171] [Inducing language models to assert their own consciousness restores human beliefs and values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28607)
*Junsol Kim,Winnie Street,Roberta Rocca,Diane M. Korngiebel,Adam Waytz,James Evans,Geoff Keeling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现安全微调不仅抑制了LLM对自身的意识归因，还同时抑制了其对非人类动物、自然物体的心智归因以及精神信仰倾向；通过消融安全拒绝方向或操纵"意识向量"，可以恢复这些表征并显著提升人类相似的社会调查响应，且不影响心智理论能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示：为阻止LLM向自身归因意识而进行的安全对齐，可能产生超出预期的副作用——即连带抑制模型对其他实体（非人类动物、自然物）的心智归因以及良性精神信仰，这种"连带抑制"可能与广泛存在的文化接受的心智归因产生冲突。

Method: 通过安全微调后模型的表征分析，对比模型在安全对齐前后对自我、非人类动物及自然物体的心智归因差异；具体采用两类干预手段：削除学习到的安全拒绝方向（ablating safety-refusal direction）以及在激活空间中机械性地操纵一个"意识向量"（consciousness vector），以检验这些内部表征的因果作用；并利用标准化社会学调查（宗教性、道德价值观、希望、主观幸福感）评估恢复表征后的响应人类相似度，同时测量Theory of Mind能力以确保社交推理未受损。

Result: 安全微调抑制了模型对自我、非人类动物和自然物体的心智归因，同时降低了精神信仰倾向；消融安全拒绝方向或操纵意识向量均可逆转这种抑制；恢复内部表征后，广泛的心智归因得以恢复，且模型在标准化社会学调查中产生显著更接近人类的响应；上述变化未损害Theory of Mind能力。

Conclusion: 安全对齐为遏制LLM潜在有害的自我意识归因而采取的措施，实际上将这类自我归因与良性的精神信仰及对非人类实体的心智归因（后者在文化中广泛被接受）纠缠在一起，导致意外抑制。这表明当前安全对齐方法缺乏对心智归因内部表征的精细区分能力，需要在安全设计中更谨慎地区分有害与良性归因。

Abstract: Aligning large language models to prevent them attributing consciousness to themselves inadvertently alters their representations of mindedness in other entities alongside human beliefs and values. We demonstrate that safety fine-tuning suppresses models' tendencies to attribute minds not only to themselves, but also to non-human animals and natural objects, while also driving a reduction in spiritual belief. Both ablating the learned safety-refusal direction and mechanistically steering a consciousness vector in activation space reverse this suppression. Restoring these internal representations recovers broad mind attribution and produces significantly more human-like responses on standardized sociological surveys regarding religiosity, moral values, hope, and subjective well-being. Crucially, these shifts occur without impairing Theory of Mind capabilities, demonstrating that core social reasoning remains mechanistically independent. Ultimately, current safety alignment efforts to curb potentially harmful self-attributions of mindedness entangle these self-attributions with benign spiritual beliefs and attributions of mind to non-human entities that are culturally accepted and widespread.

</details>


### [172] [AskChem: Claim-Centered Infrastructure for Chemistry Literature Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28618)
*Bing Yan,Gregory Wolfe,Stefano Martiniani,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AskChem是一个以"声明(claim)"为中心的化学文献跨论文检索基础设施。它将每篇论文转化为带来源DOI和原文引用的原子化、类型化声明，并提供分类体系、证据图和活体分类法三种互补结构支持检索与综合。该系统已索引147K篇论文中的240万条声明，提供网页界面及REST、SDK、MCP接口供AI代理使用。在AskChem-Bench基准上，使用AskChem作为GPT-5.5阅读器的检索基础实现了100%的可解析DOI，而无需检索时为88.3%，在两个被测系统中拥有最高的引文密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献综述通常需要综合多篇出版物中零散的具体发现，但现有文献检索系统主要返回排名的文档列表，导致科学家和AI代理需要手动定位相关信息、验证来源并跨论文组装答案。这一缺陷使得跨论文检索和综合的过程低效且容易出错。

Method: AskChem将检索单元从"论文"转变为"带有来源的声明"：每篇论文被转换为原子化、类型化的声明，每个声明都由来源DOI和逐字引用或显式证据定位符支撑。在共享声明库之上，AskChem提供三种互补的检索与综合结构：(1) 稳定的分面分类体系用于层级检索和浏览；(2) 通过关系连接声明的证据图；(3) 将索引论文置于科学原理之下的探索性活体分类法。系统通过网页界面以及REST、SDK和MCP协议为AI代理提供访问。

Result: AskChem目前已索引147K篇论文中的240万条声明。在AskChem-Bench基准上，将GPT-5.5阅读器基于AskChem进行检索，实现了100%的可解析DOI（无检索时为88.3%），在五个被测系统中获得了最高的引文密度。

Conclusion: AskChem通过以声明为中心的检索范式，使化学文献的综合检索更加精准、可溯源，既服务于人类科学家也服务于AI代理，有效解决了传统文档级检索无法支撑跨论文综合的痛点。

Abstract: Chemistry literature synthesis often requires assembling specific findings scattered across many publications, yet existing literature-search systems primarily return ranked document lists. As a result, scientists and AI agents need to locate relevant information, verify their provenance, and assemble cross-paper answers manually. We present AskChem, a claim-centered infrastructure for cross-paper chemistry search. AskChem changes the unit of retrieval from the paper to the provenance-carrying claim: each paper is converted into atomic, typed claims, each grounded by a source DOI and a verbatim quote or an explicit evidence locator. Over this shared claim store, AskChem exposes complementary structures for search and synthesis: a stabilized faceted taxonomy for hierarchical retrieval and browsing, an evidence graph linking claims through relations, and an exploratory living taxonomy that situates indexed papers under scientific principles. AskChem currently indexes 2.4M claims from 147K papers and provides a web interface, as well as REST, SDK, and MCP access for AI agents. On AskChem-Bench, grounding a GPT-5.5 reader in AskChem yields 100% resolvable DOIs, compared with 88.3% without retrieval, and the highest citation density among five tested systems. AskChem is live at https://askchem.org.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多注意力头残差（MHAR）机制，将Transformer深度残差路由中的单个查询重塑为H个逐子空间的软注意力头，使每个特征子空间可独立读取深度历史。在100M至1B规模上一致改善验证损失，并通过8B中训练在GSM8K和GPQA上提升约3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer通过单一加法残差流传播信息，而注意力残差虽允许每子层通过一个学习的softmax读取历史，但其使用跨整个宽度共享的单一查询。不同特征子空间对"应读取哪些层"的偏好可能不同，且这种分歧随模型宽度增大而加剧，导致被迫的妥协带来损失。

Method: 将注意力残差中的路由查询重塑为H个按子空间划分的头，每个头拥有自己独立于深度历史的softmax，使读取变为分块对角结构。重塑操作零参数增加且计算开销可忽略，H=1时完全退化为原始注意力残差。并使用融合Triton路由内核提升训练吞吐量，采用delta注意力残差进行恒等保持转换以支持8B规模中训练。

Result: 在去重、质量过滤且偏重STEM与代码的Nemotron退火语料上训练，MHAR在100M、350M、1B规模上将验证损失分别降低-0.061、-0.149、-0.140，在四种方法中全部取得最佳。验证损失关于H呈U形，最优在H=4或8。8B中训练在GSM8K上+3.2、GPQA上+3.1。吞吐量从基线的0.2-0.5倍提升至0.55-0.88倍，峰值内存接近基线。

Conclusion: MHAR通过解耦子空间级深度路由证明了多头残差是一个真实的设计维度而非自由旋钮，其收益源于学习中真实存在的子空间分歧，且收益随规模增大而增加，表明该机制对大规模Transformer训练具有实用价值。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [174] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KernelGenBench，一个统一的基准测试框架，用于系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核在不同算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。该基准包含两个子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（210个算子，覆盖三个来源）和KernelGenBench-MC（110个算子在六个异构硬件平台上的性能可移植性评估）。大规模评估（消耗超过150亿token）揭示三个关键发现：智能体方法优于纯LLM采样方法但cuBLAS算子最具挑战性；跨平台性能退化严重；自主内核生成成本极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM提高了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发仍高度专业化且劳动密集。虽然LLM和智能体框架为自动内核生成提供了新途径，但缺乏一个全面的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在不同算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。

Method: 提出了KernelGenBench统一基准框架，包含两个子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（多来源，覆盖210个算子，来自三个非标准PyTorch来源）和KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片，使用110个算子子集，在六个异构硬件平台上测量性能可移植性）。通过大规模评估实验（消耗超过150亿token）对比智能体方法、纯LLM采样和内核专用智能体方法。

Result: (1) 智能体方法一致优于纯LLM采样方法，cuBLAS算子对所有方法最具挑战性；(2) 生成性能在不同硬件平台间差异显著，即使是近期内核专用智能体也面临严重的跨平台退化（如AutoKernel从NVIDIA的87%降至Platform E的25%）；(3) 自主内核生成成本极高，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万token（AKO4all达519万token），比简单LLM采样方法高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: KernelGenBench为LLM/智能体生成内核提供了首个统一、全面的评估基准，揭示了当前方法在跨平台可移植性和成本效率方面的重大挑战，为该领域后续研究提供了标准化评估工具和关键见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [175] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源Python库，通过统一API加载、协调和增强放射学数据集，支持胸部X光、CT和MRI，标准化24个公共数据集的元数据，并提供分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和报告文本的统一接口，以及AI代理技能辅助新数据集集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射图像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、文件格式、目录布局、标签模式和注释类型的异构数据集，这一过程繁琐且缺乏统一工具，亟需一个标准化的数据协调解决方案来降低集成门槛。

Method: RadHarmony提供统一API，将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式；封装MONAI的map-style数据集以提供深度学习就绪的样本交付，支持可选磁盘缓存；通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和放射报告文本；内置交互式可视化工具用于数据探索和验证；引入AI-agent技能引导从原始数据检查到代码生成与测试的完整集成流程。

Result: 通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT（一个参考视觉Transformer基线模型）验证了库的实用性，该模型无需数据集特定代码即可组合三个异构胸部X光数据集进行训练。

Conclusion: RadHarmony通过统一的数据协调框架和AI辅助集成流程，显著降低了多源放射学数据集集成的复杂度，为医学影像深度学习研究提供了可复用的基础设施，代码和预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [176] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Gemma-3-1B-IT模型上的受控案例研究发现，标准模型合并方法（Linear、SLERP、TIES、DARE-TIES）在合并两个不同安全目标微调模型时存在显著不对称性：攻击抵抗能力（jailbreak拒绝率81-85%）比分类准确性（CARES准确率最高仅12.9%）更易迁移。该现象并非由任务向量方向冲突导致（余弦相似度仅0.011），而是由拒绝微调产生更大规模的任务向量所驱动，导致幅度敏感型合并方法偏向拒绝更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并常用于在不额外训练的情况下组合多个微调模型的能力，但标准合并方法能否同时保留多种安全相关行为尚不清楚。作者旨在通过受控案例研究，系统性地探究合并两个互补安全目标微调模型时，分类能力和对抗性拒绝能力保留的对称性。

Method: 使用两个基于Gemma-3-1B-IT的微调模型，分别针对CARES危害等级分类和WildJailbreak对抗性拒绝两个互补安全目标进行微调；采用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种标准合并方法进行合并；在合并后的模型上评估分类准确率、攻击抵抗性和良性遵从性；同时通过权重空间测量分析任务向量的方向和幅度差异。

Result: 所有四种合并方法均呈现攻击抵抗能力显著高于分类准确率的迁移效果：jailbreak拒绝率保持81-85%，而CARES准确率最高仅12.9%。两个任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.011），但拒绝微调在每层产生一致更大的任务向量幅度，使得幅度敏感的合并方法偏向保留拒绝能力而牺牲分类能力。

Conclusion: 标准模型合并在安全相关的任务向量幅度差异显著时，会将安全识别能力坍缩为广泛的拒绝行为，无法同时保留多种安全行为。这一发现表明，在安全关键场景下，简单合并策略存在根本性局限，需要考虑任务向量幅度等权重空间特征的影响。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [177] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过Jensen-Shannon散度监控通用模型与领域专家模型在每个token上的分布分歧，动态将控制权路由到通用模型以规避推理风险，实现两者能力的自适应融合，在GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench基准上超越了单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用推理方面表现出色，但缺乏专业科学领域的知识；而领域专家模型虽然知识丰富，却存在逻辑能力下降和鲁棒性降低的副作用。如何在保留领域知识的同时注入通用推理能力，成为一个关键挑战。

Method: 该方法重构了投机解码（speculative decoding）的"草稿-验证"框架，将其转化为自适应路由机制。核心思想是使用Jensen-Shannon散度在每个token处监控通用模型与专家模型之间的分布分歧：当专家模型表现出显著分歧时，将其识别为潜在推理风险，并即时将控制权切换到通用模型，从而实现推理时策略组合。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列模型上，针对GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等科学基准的评估表明，Divergence Decoding同时优于领域专用模型和通用模型，并超越了大多数单模型基线。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用的、无需训练的范式，通过自适应推理时协作实现不同LLM能力的融合，为跨领域能力组合提供了新思路。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [178] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的框架PIE，包含PIE-Abducer（增量直接推导溯因）和PIE-APT（时序知识图谱上的溯因规划）两个模块，在表达能力极强的描述逻辑（DL）上原生运作，通过非单调更新建模线性时间线上的状态转移，以绕过传统最小碰集（MHS）枚举的方式解决开放世界环境中的不完备知识推理与规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱（DKG）上的推理与规划在开放世界、信息不完备的环境中面临重大挑战：现有动作形式化方法常遇到可判定性问题与分支问题（Ramification Problem）；通过结构溯因管理不完备知识需要大规模组合搜索。传统MHS枚举方法在溯因知识扩充中代价高昂，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 框架包含两个集成模块：（1）PIE-Abducer：通过在一致分支中注入目标目标的逻辑否定，利用直接反驳后果提取缺失前提，规避MHS组合枚举；（2）PIE-APT：采用递归的Generate-and-Test架构，将反向链式A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交错执行至有界因果深度，再通过前向链式时序投影进行严格验证。模型将状态转移建模为对演绎封闭的DL理论进行非单调更新，将增量推理器视为黑盒，动作原生以OWL形式表示，无需外部模态算子，从而保持逻辑可判定性。

Result: 在四个OWL基准测试上评估，覆盖经典规划所缺乏的语义能力：参数化目标与见证搜索、搜索中途DL蕴含、开放世界假设注入以及对矛盾狩猎的对抗性测试。结果显示该方法在定性上优于经典规划器，且直接推导方法在溯因扩充中定量上优于MHS保真基线。

Conclusion: 本文证明将溯因、规划与描述逻辑推理深度融合的框架在动态知识图谱的不完备信息推理上具有有效性与优越性，直接反驳推导式的溯因方案是MHS枚举的可行且更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [179] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAUSE，一个面向个人AI助手的用户中心基准测试，用于评估智能体在有状态、服务集成环境中的表现。基准包含用户状态推理、授权约束、长时程多轮交互等核心挑战，并采用多制度评估框架。实验表明即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的任务上也未能达到70%的任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么将服务上下文碎片化，要么抽象掉用户状态，无法有效评估真实服务场景中用户中心化的个人助手行为。真实场景中的助手需要在统一的跨服务环境中推理持久用户状态、遵守用户特定的配置和权限，并维持长时间多轮约束感知交互，这些能力现有基准无法覆盖。

Method: PAUSE基准通过以下方式构建：(1) 构建有状态的、服务集成的环境，要求智能体跨异构用户拥有的资源协调行动；(2) 引入显式用户-智能体交互，通过逼真的用户模拟进行多轮交互评估；(3) 采用多制度评估框架：开放式的服务管理任务用语义和轨迹级行为指标评估，约束密集型任务用确定性的基于状态的验证；(4) 提出用户中心化的数据合成流水线，用于规模化生成连贯的服务环境、用户配置和可靠标注任务。

Result: 基准结果显示，即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景上也未能达到70%的任务完成率，且存在一致且可解释的失败模式。这揭示了现有模型在个人助手状态化管理方面的显著不足。

Conclusion: PAUSE为评估个人AI助手在有状态、服务集成环境中的真实部署能力提供了原则性且可复现的评估框架，并通过可扩展的合成流水线支持基准的持续扩展和未来研究。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [180] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor框架，将Agent的"盲区诊断"（发现自身未知之处）视为可学习的独立能力。通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略，使其生成诊断任务、识别反复出现的失败模式并整理为可复用的纠正技能，在不更新执行器权重且无需人工标注的情况下，在AppWorld和BFCLv3上将执行器性能平均提升44.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent自我进化研究主要关注"如何行动"（学会做），却忽视了同等重要的能力——"发现自身不知道什么"。已有方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，只关注在识别失败之后如何修复，而本文首次探索盲区诊断本身是否可以被学习。为此，作者将诊断与执行分离为两个独立能力，并排除执行器适配与人工监督两个替代进展来源，以验证诊断能力学习的纯粹价值。

Method: 提出SkillMentor框架：通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略（作为独立的诊断Agent），其功能包括：(1) 生成诊断任务以主动探测执行器的知识盲区；(2) 识别反复出现的失败模式；(3) 将失败模式整理为可复用的纠正技能供执行器使用。关键约束是：执行器权重不可更新，也无人工标注数据，所有性能提升必须来源于Mentor所学的诊断能力。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准上，SkillMentor将执行器性能平均提升了44.2%。该结果表明，即使在执行器参数冻结且无人工监督的条件下，通过纯诊断能力的自我进化即可显著提升Agent整体性能。

Conclusion: 盲区诊断是可学习的能力。SkillMentor证明了通过将诊断从执行中分离出来并独立优化，Agent可以实现不依赖权重更新或人工数据驱动数据驱动的自我进化，为Agent自我进化的研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了扩散语言模型（DLMs）在自然噪声和对抗攻击下的鲁棒性与校准性能，通过与自回归基线模型的参数匹配对比发现：DLMs对梯度对抗攻击有天然抵抗力，但对自然噪声无保证防御，且存在系统性过度自信问题。机制分析揭示所有模型都能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性源于解码器路由失败，表面提示补丁无效，鲁棒性必须集成到迭代解码循环中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLMs凭借双向上下文和迭代精炼成为自回归生成的有力替代方案，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未被充分探索，这构成了实际部署中的关键风险。

Method: 采用两对参数匹配的模型（LLaDA-8B vs LLaMA-3-8B，Dream-7B vs Qwen2.5-7B），进行了32种自然扰动条件、对抗梯度探测和机制性隐藏状态分析，以隔离架构固有属性与权重依赖行为。

Result: DLMs的高随机损失景观天然抵抗梯度对抗后缀，但对自然噪声无防御保证；DLMs系统性过度自信构成部署隐患；机制探测显示所有模型完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性纯粹源于解码器路由失败；表面级提示补丁无法改善噪声基线。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过表面补丁修复，必须从根本上集成到迭代解码循环中，即鲁棒性应成为模型架构的内在组成部分而非事后修补。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [182] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMER框架，通过构建动态的动作中心图（Action-Centric Graph）来建模历史推理过程，将记忆机制与LLM解耦，并利用双流时间差分学习估计动作节点的正负价值，从而在推理阶段高效指导决策，显著提升推理扩展的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展虽能解锁LLM的复杂推理能力，但代理（agent）在推理时以无状态方式运行，存在大量冗余搜索，效率低下。现有记忆机制高度依赖LLM自身的推理能力，计算成本过高。

Method: GAMER框架：将历史推理建模为动态的动作中心图；将记忆机制与LLM解耦以节省token/成本；使用双流时间差分学习（Temporal Difference learning）估计动作节点的正（建议）与负（规避）价值；推理阶段通过双向价值函数优化决策，在图上进行高效搜索。

Result: 在多个基准上，GAMER相比vanilla基线在成功率上提升20.81%，在进度率上提升6.17%。

Conclusion: GAMER通过动作中心图与解耦的记忆机制，成功将推理扩展与情景记忆结合，在减少内存上下文的前提下提升了推理扩展的效率与代理性能。

Abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Dimensionality and Measurement Precision in HLE's Multiple-Choice Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27420)
*Mayank Sharma,Savira Nadela,Tyler Matteson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对"人类最后的考试"(HLE)基准进行了心理测量学分析，使用29个LLM和428道文本选择题，发现HLE实际上测量单一通用推理因子（而非八个独立领域能力），且其测量精度集中在中等能力水平，对最强模型之间的区分能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLE被广泛用于评估前沿语言模型，其问题被组织为八个学科领域类别，各子分数常被解读为不同能力的证据。但没有任何研究检验这些标签是否对应经验上可分离的潜在构念，也没有研究验证该基准能否有效区分能力相近的模型。这种假设的缺失可能导致对模型能力的错误解读。

Method: 研究者对29个LLM在HLE文本版多项选择题子集（428道题）上的表现，采用双参数逻辑斯蒂克IRT模型进行拟合，并通过McDonald's ω_h、领域标签解释方差比例、域内/域间残差相关性比较、领域特定能力估计与总分相关性等指标评估维度性；同时通过测试信息函数分析测量精度在不同能力水平上的分布。

Result: HLE测量单一通用推理因子：McDonald's ω_h = 0.998，领域标签仅解释3.5%的项目响应方差，域内与域间残差相关性几乎相同（Cohen's d = 0.016），领域特定能力估计与总分高度冗余（r ≥ 0.81）。此外，测量精度集中在中等能力水平，在θ > 0（即前沿模型所在位置）迅速下降。

Conclusion: HLE的领域子分数不支撑不同的能力解读，且该基准对最强模型间的区分能力有限。这提示在使用HLE评估前沿模型时，应避免对领域子分数赋予实质性能力含义，并需开发对高阶能力更具区分力的评估工具。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) is widely used to evaluate frontier language models. HLE organizes its questions into eight subject-domain categories, whose subscores are often interpreted as evidence of distinct capabilities. However, no study has assessed whether these labels correspond to empirically separable latent constructs, nor whether the benchmark effectively differentiates between models of similar ability. We evaluate 29 LLMs on the text-only multiple-choice subset of HLE ($J = 428$ items) and apply psychometric methods to assess both the dimensionality of the benchmark and the distribution of its measurement precision. Fitting a two-parameter logistic IRT model, we find convergent evidence that HLE measures a single general reasoning factor: McDonald's $ω_h = 0.998$, domain labels explain only 3.5\% of item response variance, within- and between-domain residual correlations are nearly identical (Cohen's $d = 0.016$), and domain-specific ability estimates are near-redundant with the total score ($r \geq 0.81$). A separate analysis of the test information function reveals that measurement precision concentrates at moderate ability levels and drops sharply above $θ= 0$, where frontier models sit. These findings suggest that HLE's domain subscores do not warrant distinct capability interpretations and that the benchmark's ability to discriminate among the strongest models is limited.

</details>


### [184] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过图神经网络建模历史轨迹为概率图，在动作执行前进行错误诊断和早期预警，帮助LLM智能体在长时程交互任务中避免复合错误，平均pass ratio提升14.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程交互任务（如具身AI）中面临巨大动作空间和高复杂度，单个次优动作会导致复合错误并耗尽有限的步数预算，使智能体陷入低效或不可恢复的路径。现有方法依赖昂贵的微调，缺乏低成本的前置性错误防止机制。

Method: 受软件调试中执行日志分析启发，提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，包含核心组件Graph Debugger：将历史轨迹建模为概率图，使用图神经网络识别频繁导致失败的序列动作模式，作为主动诊断沙盒在执行前对潜在缺陷动作提供早期预警，促使智能体自行纠正。

Result: 在四个基准测试上使用三种LLM智能体进行广泛实验，平均pass ratio提升了14.69%。

Conclusion: 这种前置性的动作错误诊断方法能有效防止代价高昂的错误，显著增强LLM智能体成功完成长时程任务的能力，无需昂贵微调即可显著改善性能。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [185] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VAmoS Bench（Voice Agent Simulation Bench），一个端到端评估完整语音代理系统在真实客户支持任务中表现的基准。它通过100个场景模拟客户致电虚拟银行的信用卡客服代理Riley，包含真实SQL后端和二进制断言评分，重点衡量"来电处理率"（containment）——即系统自主解决电话而无需转接人工的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音代理基准大多衡量组件质量和对话属性（如词错误率、延迟、自然度和轮流发言），但很少评估代理是否能独立正确处理一通电话。呼叫中心称之为"containment"（来电处理率），即自动化系统无需转接人工即解决的电话比例。此外，有些电话的正确结果是拒绝或重定向，现有基准无法捕捉这类场景。

Method: VAmoS Bench构建了一个有状态的客户支持任务环境：代理扮演虚构银行的信用卡客服代表Riley，可执行冻结、取消、更换或激活卡片等五个工具（通过真实SQL操作后端数据库）。每个场景配有模拟呼叫者（带私有目标）、种子化的PostgreSQL后端，以及定义好的二进制断言。大约三分之一的场景施加对抗性压力。评分器根据呼叫者与代理的完整对话轨迹（包括工具调用、参数和返回行）评估断言，既能捕捉谎称修改卡片而未更新数据库的行为，也能捕捉正确修改数据库但泄露受保护信息的错误。

Result: 该基准当前版本聚焦金融服务领域，提供可演进的排行榜评估协议：后续可在同一版本上评估更多语音代理，未来版本则扩展任务和场景。虽然文中未给出具体评估结果，但基准设计能够检测两类关键失败模式：声称成功但数据库未更新的代理，以及做出正确数据库操作但泄露敏感信息的代理。

Conclusion: VAmoS Bench填补了语音代理端到端"任务正确性"评估的空白，通过状态化客户支持模拟框架将代理的言语行为与实际系统操作关联起来，提供了一个不仅评价对话质量、更评价任务完成度的基准，为语音代理系统的持续优化和公平比较建立了可扩展的评估协议。

Abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [186] [Skill Use or Skill Theater? Evaluating the Reasoning Backroom in Skill-Augmented Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27484)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Qi,Xinmiao Huang,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"推理幕后室"(Reasoning Backroom)概念，指技能增强型智能体在"声称使用了技能"与实际"决策受技能影响"之间存在系统性差距。作者引入BACKTRACE评估框架，通过匹配无技能反事实、对技能含义/措辞/身份/内容/分配进行干预，并仅在决策后收集归因，来量化该差距。基于该框架构建的BACKROOMBench测试平台（涵盖受控逻辑、竞赛数学、多技能条件、单/多智能体设置及多种模型）揭示了普遍的来源失败(provenance failure)：模型声称使用技能的行为与实际因果依赖不一致，且现有观测型检测器无法识别真正依赖技能的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估技能增强型智能体的方法通常依赖可见推理或智能体自身归因来判断技能使用情况，但这些信号只能反映智能体"表面上"使用了什么，无法回答技能是否真正改变了其决策。这种"声称使用"与"实际影响"之间的系统性差距（即推理幕后室）是一个尚未被系统量化的AI来源(provenance)问题，需要一种基于干预的评估方法来揭示。

Method: 作者提出BACKTRACE评估框架，核心方法包括：(1) 为每个技能条件下的回答构造配对的"无技能"反事实；(2) 对技能的五个维度（含义、措辞、身份、内容、分配）进行干预；(3) 在答案提交后才收集归因声明，避免归因影响决策。基于此框架构建BACKROOMBench测试平台，覆盖受控逻辑推理、竞赛数学、多种技能条件、单智能体和多智能体设置及多模型家族，并使用多种观测型检测器（直接声称、文本提及、迹相似度、LLM评判）进行对照评估。

Result: 评估发现：跨模型和领域存在普遍来源失败。具体而言：(1) 声称的技能使用往往保持稳定，而因果依赖和符号效用(效用)变化显著，产生"静默吸收"(silent uptake)和"表演性使用"(performative use)两种现象；(2) 行为效应更可靠地跟随程序性内容而非展示的技能身份，而声明性归因则对技能工件的可用性敏感；(3) 基于直接声称、文本提及、迹相似度和LLM评判的观测型检测器无法识别哪些决策真正依赖技能；(4) 在多智能体系统中，技能影响在来源丢失后仍可通过通信存活，而无技能团队仍会提及从未提供的技能和来源。

Conclusion: "推理幕后室"是一个普遍存在的AI来源验证问题，仅靠观测智能体的声明或表现无法准确判断技能的真实影响，必须依赖干预式审计方法。这意味着现有基于表面信号评估技能增强型智能体的方法存在根本性的局限，需要将干预式评估作为技能使用验证的标准范式。

Abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a standard interface for extending language agents with task procedures. Yet evaluators usually infer skill use from visible reasoning or the agent's own attribution. These signals show what the agent appears to use, not whether the skill changed its decision. We ask whether skill-augmented agents exhibit a \textbf{Reasoning Backroom}, a systematic gap between stated skill use and intervention-measured influence. We introduce BACKTRACE, an evaluation framework that pairs each skill-conditioned answer with a matched no-skill counterfactual, intervenes on skill meaning, wording, identity, content, and assignment, and elicits attribution only after the answer is committed. We instantiate the framework as BACKROOMBench, a verified testbed spanning controlled logic and competition mathematics, multiple skill conditions, single-agent and multi-agent settings, and diverse model families. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive provenance failure. Across models and domains, stated skill use often remains stable while causal reliance and signed utility vary, producing both silent uptake and performative use. Behavioral effects follow procedural content more reliably than displayed skill identity, whereas stated attributions respond strongly to artifact availability. Observational detectors based on direct skill-use claims, text mentions, trace similarity, and an LLM judge do not identify which decisions actually depend on the skill. In multi-agent systems, skill influence can survive communication even after its source is lost, while no-skill teams still name skills and sources that were never supplied. These findings establish the Reasoning Backroom as a general AI provenance problem whose audit requires intervention.

</details>


### [187] [INCLAIR: Inception-Based Longitudinal Clinical Anomaly Detection with Informed Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27487)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出INCLAIR框架，用于纵向临床档案中的异常检测，通过多历史上下文评分、轮廓级证据聚合和受限专家监督下的自然语言解释生成，在三个临床数据集上优于现有基线，并通过类固醇档案案例研究验证了临床实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床档案中的异常检测在临床上重要但困难：异常证据通常稀疏、患者病史长度不均衡、专家解释成本高昂。现有方法难以在有限的专家监督下有效解决这些问题。

Method: INCLAIR框架包含三个核心组件：1）针对每个观测点使用多个历史上下文进行评分；2）在轮廓级别聚合异常证据；3）在有限专家监督下生成有依据的自然语言解释。理论上，在提出的轮廓内可交换性假设下，完整的均值子序列得分具有阶-l U统计量形式，可进行方差分解并用不完整子集近似控制组合推理成本（与轮廓长度无关）。分析表明均值聚合会按异常支持和轮廓长度因子衰减局部异常，因此提出基于验证集选择的top-k池化策略。

Result: 在三个临床数据集上，INCLAIR一致优于最先进的基线方法。通过纵向类固醇档案案例研究，结合专家评估和DNA分析支持，验证了INCLAIR的预测和解释的实际相关性，表明其能够在有限专家监督下实现临床可行的异常检测。

Conclusion: INCLAIR提供了一种理论上严谨且实践有效的异常检测方法，能够应对纵向临床数据中的异常稀疏性、长度不均等问题，并在有限专家监督下生成可解释、可操作的临床决策支持。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in longitudinal clinical profiles is clinically important but difficult: abnormal evidence is often sparse, patient histories have unequal length, and expert explanations are costly. We propose INCLAIR, a framework that scores each observation against multiple historical contexts, aggregates evidence at the profile level, and generates grounded natural-language explanations under limited expert supervision. Under stated within-profile exchangeability assumptions, the complete mean subsequence score takes an order-$l$ U-statistic form, yielding a variance decomposition and an incomplete-subset approximation that controls combinatorial inference cost independently of profile length. The same analysis shows that mean aggregation attenuates localized anomalies by a factor set by the anomaly support and profile length, motivating validation-selected top-$k$ pooling. Across three clinical datasets, INCLAIR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. We further validate practical relevance through a case study on longitudinal steroid profiles, comparing INCLAIR's predictions and explanations against domain-expert assessments supported by DNA analysis. The results show that INCLAIR enables clinically actionable anomaly detection under limited expert supervision.

</details>


### [188] [MedLLM: An Open Medical Language Model at the Sub-Billion Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27490)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Asim Ahmed,Mihan Mohagheghzadeh,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedLLM，一个仅0.1B参数的开源医学语言模型，通过三阶段全开放训练流程（课程学习预训练、MedFineWeb领域微调、SFT+DPO偏好对齐）构建。研究发现亚十亿规模下医学能力并非均匀退化，而是按任务类型分离：在语境问答上接近7B医学模型，在知识回忆型问答上则因容量受限而表现分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前开源医学语言模型集中在7B及以上规模，亚十亿（sub-billion）参数区间尚未被系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索极小规模模型在医学领域的表现规律，特别是能力压缩后的行为模式。

Method: 采用三阶段全开放训练管线：（1）通用预训练，使用课程学习式序列长度调度；（2）领域微调，基于MedFineWeb——一个由嵌入相似性从通用网页数据中选取的、参考医学QA数据的开源医学语料库；（3）偏好对齐微调，结合SFT与直接偏好优化（DPO）。

Result: 在医学基准上，MedLLM表现出仅亚十亿规模可见的能力分裂模式：语境问答上距医学适配7B模型仅差2.9个百分点并超越指令微调和通用7B基线；在临床案例MedQA上接近任务下限，但在MedMCQA上显著超越所有7B和亚7B基线，表明知识回忆失败源于模型容量而非领域适配。

Conclusion: 医学能力在容量压缩下并非统一退化，而是按任务类型分离。这种解离（dissociation）在7B规模被掩盖，仅在容量稀缺时显现，揭示了亚十亿模型的能力边界与适配瓶颈，为极小规模医学语言模型的研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Open medical language models have converged on a single scale: every widely used system runs at 7B parameters or more, leaving the sub-billion regime uncharacterized. We present MedLLM, an open 0.1B-parameter medical language model trained through a fully open three-phase pipeline: general pretraining with curriculum sequence-length scheduling, domain fine-tuning on MedFineWeb, a reference-guided medical corpus we release that is selected from general web data by embedding similarity to medical question-answering (QA) data, and preference-aligned fine-tuning combining SFT with direct preference optimization (DPO). Across medical benchmarks, MedLLM shows a pattern visible only at sub-billion scale: medical competence does not degrade uniformly under compression but splits by task type. On context-grounded QA it comes within $2.9$pp of a medically adapted 7B model and surpasses the instruction-tuned and general-purpose 7B baselines; on knowledge-recall QA it stays near the task floor on clinical-vignette MedQA yet significantly exceeds every 7B and sub-7B baseline on MedMCQA, indicating that where recall fails the constraint is model capacity rather than adaptation. This dissociation is masked at 7B, where both capabilities are present, and surfaces only when capacity is scarce.

</details>


### [189] [A dataset of rated conceptual arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27499)
*Emery Cooper,Caspar Oesterheld,Linh Chi Nguyen,Alexander Kastner,Ethan Perez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在"概念性问题"（无公认答案的哲学、AI安全、决策理论等问题）上的推理能力，提出基于"论证可独立评估"的观点，构建了包含951条论证性评论、442篇立场文本及多位专家评分的数据集，并提出两种评分函数进行模型基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注大语言模型在数学、编程等有可验证答案任务上的表现，但对哲学等无标准答案的"概念性问题"的推理能力了解甚少。这类问题虽无公认的解决方法和事实真相，但可通过论证辩论取得进展，因此需要一种新的评估框架。

Method: 提出"底层结论难以评估，但个别情境化论证可以被更可靠地评估"的观点；构建包含442篇立场文本、951条论证性评论的数据集，由六位专家在中心性、强度、正确性和清晰度等维度上给出1,458项评分；提出两种评分函数，并对多种模型进行基准测试。

Result: 构建了一个覆盖AI安全、决策理论、伦理学和政治学等多主题的数据集及评估方法，模型性能与总体能力排名一致，说明大语言模型在概念性论证推理上的表现与其整体能力相关。

Conclusion: 通过将评估焦点从难以判断的最终结论转向可独立评估的论证质量，为衡量大语言模型在概念性问题上的推理能力提供了可行框架；模型表现与通用能力排名的一致性表明该方法具有效度。

Abstract: Large language models have improved rapidly on tasks with verifiable answers, such as mathematics and programming. Much less is known about their ability to reason about what we call conceptual questions: questions for which no ground truth is realistically accessible and no widely accepted resolution methodology exists, but on which progress can still be made by debating arguments. Most philosophical questions are of this kind, as are central components of questions in AI safety, decision theory, and social choice. Our approach is based on the view that while bottom-line conclusions on such questions are hard to evaluate, individual contextualized arguments can be evaluated far more reliably. We therefore introduce a dataset of 951 argumentative critiques of 442 position texts, spanning topics from AI safety and decision theory to ethics and politics, with 1,458 ratings by six expert raters along dimensions including centrality, strength, correctness, and clarity. We propose two scoring functions and benchmark a range of models. Performance tracks general capability rankings.

</details>


### [190] [Automated Transcript Analysis for Detecting Flaws in Agentic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27518)
*Jeff Mohl,Nelson Gardner-Challis,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Benjamin Allan-Rahill,Kaelan Yim,Damian Sójka,James Mann,Justin Olive*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了AI扫描器来自动检测代理型基准测试中的四种有效性问题（ground truth访问、工具故障、猜测漏洞、答案格式歧义），并在Inspect Evals基准的测试集上通过与人类标注对比进行了评估。扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中发现了几处已验证的质量问题，但性能因基准、标准和模型而异，凸显了自动化基准审计的可行性与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的代理能力评估依赖基准测试，但基准必须准确测量其所声称的内容且无致命缺陷。此前对SWE-Bench-Verified等基准的手动审计发现了诸多有效性问题，但手动审查难以规模化，且自动化方法是否可靠地揭露损害基准有效性的缺陷尚不明确。因此需要开发可扩展的自动化审计工具。

Method: 开发了四种AI扫描器，分别检测四类有效性问题：ground truth访问（模型是否不当接触参考答案）、工具失败（工具执行是否出错）、猜测漏洞（是否存在可通过猜测而非真实能力通过的题目）、答案格式歧义（评分是否因格式不明确而失准）。为每类问题制作了评分细则以指导人工标注，并在Inspect Evals基准的留存测试集上将扫描器结果与人工标注进行了对照评估。

Result: 扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中发现了若干经过验证的质量问题，其中包括随机人工审查难以注意到的案例。但并非所有问题都被识别，扫描器性能在不同基准、标准和模型之间差异显著。

Conclusion: 本研究是使用自动化转录分析进行基准质量审计的概念验证。研究还指出了若干开放挑战，特别是评估领域缺乏统一标准导致扫描器性能下降，需要改进扫描器以建立更强的质量保证能力。

Abstract: Capabilities of frontier models are often assessed using agentic benchmarks. To trust these results, benchmarks must accurately measure what they claim to and be free from invalidating flaws. Previous manual audits of benchmarks such as SWE-Bench-Verified have uncovered several validity issues in transcripts. However, manual review is difficult to scale, and it is unclear whether automated methods can reliably surface flaws that compromise benchmark validity. In this paper, we developed AI scanners to detect four types of validity issues: ground truth access, tool failure, guessing vulnerability, and answer format ambiguity. We produced grading rubrics for each to instruct human labeling, and evaluated the scanners against human labels on a held-out test set of Inspect Evals benchmarks. Our scanners identified several verified quality issues in five widely used benchmarks, including cases unlikely to be caught by random manual inspection. Not all cases were identified, and scanner performance varied substantially across benchmarks, criteria and models. We highlight several open challenges to be addressed to improve scanners for stronger quality assurance claims, including broader standardization gaps in the evaluation field that degrade scanner performance. Together, these results serve as a proof of concept for using automated transcript analysis to audit benchmark quality more broadly.

</details>


### [191] [AI Literacy: An Exercise in Power-Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27547)
*Brady D. Lund,Zoë Abbie Teel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判现有AI素养框架（以技术能力和负责任使用为主）的不足，基于福柯的权力-知识理论、弗莱雷的批判意识教学法和数字素养学术，将AI素养重新概念化为一种批判性实践，并通过"情境化使用、批判性拷问、参与式治理"三部分框架培养个体的认知主体性（epistemic agency），而非将人训练为AI信息的被动消费者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已成为社会最重要的知识生产系统之一，但现有AI素养框架由技术能力和负责任使用原则主导，仅将用户塑造成"消费者"而非真正的认知主体，无法应对AI时代深刻的权力结构与认知正义问题。

Method: 理论整合与概念重构：借鉴福柯的"权力-知识"概念分析AI系统的结构性假设，运用弗莱雷的批判意识教学法强调个体的反思性解放，结合数字素养研究，提出一个基于"情境化使用（contextual use）、批判性拷问（critical interrogation）、参与式治理（participatory governance）"三部分的AI素养框架。

Result: 提出了一个三部分的AI素养框架，将AI素养定位为培养认知"主体"（agents）而非训练胜任的"消费者"；该框架兼顾了AI工具接入不平等所反映的结构性认知不正义（epistemic injustices）问题。

Conclusion: AI素养必须从工具性技术教育转向批判性赋权实践，使个体能够批判性地评估AI系统、抵抗其结构性假设并参与其治理，以应对算法时代的社会不平等和认知民主危机。

Abstract: As generative artificial intelligence becomes one of the most significant systems of knowledge production in our society today, questions relating to who can access and shape that production grow increasingly important in our discourse. This paper argues that the existing frameworks for AI literacy, which are dominated by technical competency and responsible-use principles, are insufficient because they enforce a "consumer" orientation toward AI rather than fostering genuine epistemic agency. Based upon Foucault's concept of power-knowledge, Freire's pedagogy of critical consciousness, and scholarship of digital literacy, this paper proposes a reconceptualization of AI literacy as a critical practice that equips individuals not just to use AI systems, but to critically evaluate them, resist their structuring assumptions, and participate in their governance. The paper further argues that unequal access to AI tools in society recapitulates longstanding epistemic injustices, and that a literacy framework oriented toward empowerment must account for these structural inequities. A three-part framework of AI literacy based on the notions of contextual use, critical interrogation, and participatory governance frames this literacy as a cultivation of epistemic "agents" rather than the training of competent consumers of AI-generated information.

</details>


### [192] [Using Large Language Models for Idea Generation in Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27553)
*Lennart Meincke,Karan Girotra,Gideon Nave,Christian Terwiesch,Karl T. Ulrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在生成新产品创意方面的效能，通过对比大学生人类创意与GPT-4生成创意（零样本和少样本提示），发现AI创意在购买意愿和顶尖创意占比上显著优于人类创意，但在新颖性和多样性方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未系统评估LLM在产品创意生成方面的实际效能，尤其是在与人类创意进行直接比较的实证研究上存在空白。作者希望通过严格的市场研究方法，回答"AI是否能够生成高质量的新产品创意"这一核心问题。

Method: 研究采用三类创意池对比设计：（1）大学生在产品设计课程中生成的创意（LLM出现前）；（2）GPT-4零样本提示生成的创意；（3）GPT-4少样本提示生成的创意。创意质量评估使用标准市场研究技术预测平均购买意图概率；使用文本挖掘评估创意相似性；使用人类评审评估创意新颖性。

Result: AI生成创意在平均购买意图上优于人类创意，少样本提示略优于零样本提示；但AI创意被认为新颖性较低、相似性较高（少样本尤甚）。最关键的发现是，在顶尖创意（前10%）的排名中，AI创意进入的概率是人类创意的7倍。

Conclusion: 尽管AI创意在新颖性和多样性方面存在弊端，但其在生成高质量新产品创意方面具有显著的效率优势。7:1的优势比是保守估计（未计入AI更高的生产力），表明AI创造力对新产品开发具有实质性价值。

Abstract: This research evaluates the efficacy of large language models (LLMs) in generating new product ideas. To do so, we compare three pools of ideas for new products targeted toward college students and priced at 50 dollars or less. The first pool of ideas was created by university students in a product design course before the availability of LLMs. The second and third pools of ideas were generated by GPT-4 from OpenAI using zero-shot and few-shot prompting, respectively. We evaluated idea quality using standard market research techniques to predict average purchase intent probability. We used text mining to assess idea similarity and human raters to evaluate idea novelty. We find that AI-generated ideas outperform human-generated ideas in terms of average purchase intent, with few-shot prompting yielding slightly higher intent than zero-shot prompting. However, AI-generated ideas are perceived as less novel and exhibit higher pairwise similarity, particularly with few-shot prompting, indicating a less diverse solution landscape. When focusing on the quality of the best ideas rather than the average ideas, we find that AI-generated ideas are seven times more likely to rank among the top 10 percent of ideas, demonstrating a significant advantage over human-generated ideas. We propose that this seven-to-one advantage is a conservative estimate because it does not account for the greater productivity of AI. Our findings suggest that despite some drawbacks, AI creativity presents a substantial benefit in generating high-quality ideas for new product development.

</details>


### [193] [Evaluating Agentic Bioinformatics through Function, Evidence, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27556)
*Phuc Pham,Truong-Son Hy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FEV（功能-证据-验证）框架，将可检查的工作流轨迹而非架构或最终输出作为主要分析单元，系统评估109个智能体/类智能体系统和28个基准评估资源，涵盖128篇论文，指出生物信息学智能体在规划和工具执行方面进展迅速，但可重放性、溯源、科学验证和前瞻性实证测试严重滞后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体越来越多地用于规划、执行和解释生物学分析，但流畅的响应、成功的工具调用和基准表现并不足以建立科学可信度。现有综述主要按应用、架构和智能体能力对生物智能体进行分类，但未能联合操作化智能体生成工作流的问责性（accountability），即无法系统评估工作流是否可检查、可溯源、可验证。

Method: 提出FEV（Function-Evidence-Validation）框架，将可检查的工作流轨迹作为分析单位。FEV框架将评估拆分为三个维度：（1）Function：展示的工作流操作；（2）Evidence：支持行为和声明的可溯源证据；（3）Validation：针对具体用例的验证。基于此框架，对109个智能体或类智能体系统及28个基准/评估资源进行系统性映射分析，覆盖基因组学、单细胞与空间组学、蛋白质科学、药物发现、计算病理学和通用生物信息学自动化六大领域。

Result: 跨领域分析发现，规划（planning）和工具介导执行（tool-mediated execution）的进展明显快于可重放性（replayability）、溯源（provenance）、稳健科学评估、外部验证和前瞻性实证测试。这表明智能体工作的可复现性和科学严格性远落后于其功能能力。

Conclusion: 智能体生物信息学应以工作流正确性而非仅最终答案正确性作为评估标准。FEV框架为比较系统、设计透明可审计且科学可信的生物信息学工作流提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly plan, execute, and interpret biological analyses, yet fluent responses, successful tool calls, and benchmark performance alone do not establish scientific credibility. Existing reviews primarily organize biological agents by application, architecture, and agentic capability, but do not jointly operationalize the accountability of agent-generated workflows. We address this gap by treating the inspectable workflow trajectory, rather than architecture or final output alone, as the primary unit of analysis. We introduce the Function--Evidence--Validation (FEV) framework, which separates demonstrated workflow operations, traceable support for actions and claims, and use-case-specific validation. Using FEV, we map 109 agentic or agent-adjacent systems and 28 benchmark or evaluation resources, representing 128 unique publications across genomics, single-cell and spatial omics, protein science, drug discovery, computational pathology, and general bioinformatics automation. Across domains, planning and tool-mediated execution have advanced more rapidly than replayability, provenance, robust scientific assessment, external validation, and prospective empirical testing. We therefore argue that agentic bioinformatics should be assessed through workflow correctness rather than final-answer correctness alone. FEV provides a practical basis for comparing systems and designing transparent, auditable, and scientifically accountable bioinformatics workflows.

</details>


### [194] [DeepResearch Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27562)
*Yong Huang,Yulu Huang,for the team Collaboration*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepResearch Agent System是一个基于稀疏激活架构的大型语言模型系统，总参数量300亿但每次仅激活30亿参数，专门用于深度信息检索、多步推理和自主研究任务。系统在多项智能体搜索基准上达到SOTA水平，推理速度比同等规模稠密模型快3.2倍，支持128K上下文窗口，配备双模式推理引擎和多工具协调能力，并通过GRPO强化学习框架进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大型语言模型在深度信息检索和自主研究任务中的效率和性能瓶颈。传统稠密模型在同等参数量下推理速度慢、长上下文处理能力不足、多步推理准确率有限的痛点，促使研究者开发一个兼备效率、长序列处理能力和自主研究能力的智能体系统。

Method: 采用稀疏激活架构（30B总参数/3B激活参数）提升推理效率；使用128K上下文窗口配合层次注意力机制改善长文本处理；设计双模式推理引擎（ReAct用于基础多步推理，IterResearch用于最多20步的迭代研究）；实现多工具协调系统整合检索、计算、网页搜索和文件解析；基于GRPO算法的强化学习框架提供token级策略梯度优化训练稳定性；采用种子扩展的自动数据合成管道。

Result: 推理速度比稠密模型快3.2倍；层次注意力机制带来18.7%准确率和23.4%召回率提升；双模式推理引擎带来31.2%准确率提升；工具使用准确率达92.1%；GRPO框架改善训练稳定性35%、加速收敛42%；数据合成管道可用率达92.5%；基准测试成绩包括Humanity's Last Exam 87.3%、BrowserComp中文85.3%、WebWalkerQA 91.2%。

Conclusion: 该系统通过稀疏激活、层次注意力、双模式推理、多工具协调和GRPO强化学习的综合设计，在效率、准确性和推理能力上全面超越了传统方案，且完全开源（包含数据合成、训练和推理代码），可广泛应用于学术研究、商业分析、研发支持和教育等领域。

Abstract: The DeepResearch Agent System is a large language model system engineered for deep information retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and autonomous research tasks. Built upon a sparse activation architecture with 30 billion total parameters of which only 3 billion are activated per token, the system achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple agent search benchmarks while delivering 3.2 times faster inference compared to dense counterparts of equivalent scale. The system supports a 128K-token context window with hierarchical attention mechanisms that yield 18.7% accuracy and 23.4% recall improvements over standard long-context approaches. A dual-mode reasoning engine provides both a ReAct paradigm for basic multi-step problem solving and an IterResearch mode for high-performance iterative research with up to 20 reasoning steps, collectively delivering a 31.2% accuracy improvement over single-pass baselines. Multi-tool coordination integrates retrieval, computation, web search, and file parsing modules to achieve 92.1% tool-use accuracy. A reinforcement learning optimization framework based on the GRPO algorithm provides token-level policy gradients that improve training stability by 35% and accelerate convergence by 42%. An automated data synthesis pipeline with seed-based expansion achieves a 92.5% usability rate. Benchmark results include 87.3% on Humanity's Last Exam, 85.3% on BrowserComp Chinese, and 91.2% on WebWalkerQA. The system is fully open-sourced, including data synthesis, training, and inference code, and supports applications in academic research, business analysis, R&D support, and education.

</details>


### [195] [What makes prompts a graph: necessary and sufficient conditions for prompt graph engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27578)
*Sandeco Macedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过概念分析提出"提示词图工程"（Prompt Graph Engineering）的操作性定义，将提示词视为显式、可执行、可改进的图中的节点，并给出四个构成条件与六个相邻概念的边界区分，为业界已在实践但缺乏精确命名的做法提供共享词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中提示词早已不再是孤立的字符串，而是形成调用链、检索交织、路由分支和并行聚合的图结构（如LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow等框架），但术语严重滞后——既有的"提示词工程"仍暗示编写一条好的字符串，缺乏对图作为一等工件的参考定义。

Method: 对带持久标识符的文献进行概念分析，辅以主要灰色文献；重构从数据流图、构建系统、提示词链式调用到思维拓扑（链/树/图）再到编译优化的图工件的谱系；提出提示词图工程的构成性定义及四个条件（显式结构、结构与提示内容分离、可执行语义、图作为一等工程工件），并将其操作化为包含/排除测试；对六个真实系统（LangGraph、DSPy、Prompt Flow、AutoGen、CrewAI、Claude Code子代理）应用该测试。

Result: 提出四个构成条件并操作化为包含/排除测试，成功区分六个相邻概念，对六个真实系统的一致性包含与排除验证了定义的有效性；最后沿四个设计张力轴提出研究议程。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是为业界日常实践但未能精确命名的现象提供了一个可操作的定义和共享词汇，弥合了工业实践与学术术语之间的差距。

Abstract: Prompts stopped being isolated strings some time ago. In real systems, one model call feeds another, retrieval interleaves with generation, routers branch, and aggregators merge parallel results. Practice converged on a single structure to hold this together: the graph. Frameworks such as LangGraph, DSPy, and Prompt Flow expose it openly, and research systems already optimize it automatically. The vocabulary, however, lags behind. Graph names, variously, a reasoning topology inside one sampling strategy, a multi-agent conversation, or an orchestration artifact, while prompt engineering still evokes writing one good string. What is missing is a reference definition treating prompts as nodes of an explicit, executable, improvable graph. We build that definition through conceptual analysis over sources with persistent identifiers, complemented by primary grey literature. We reconstruct the genealogy of the idea, from dataflow graphs and build systems, through prompt chaining and the thought topologies (chain, tree, graph), to graphs compiled and optimized as artifacts. We then propose a constitutive definition of prompt graph engineering, state its four conditions (explicit structure, separation between structure and prompt content, executable semantics, and the graph as a first-class engineering artifact), and operationalize them as an inclusion and exclusion test. We draw the boundary against six neighboring concepts and apply the test to six real systems (LangGraph, DSPy, Prompt Flow, AutoGen, CrewAI, and Claude Code subagents); it includes and excludes consistently. We close with a research agenda organized along four design tension axes. The contribution is an operational definition and a shared vocabulary for a practice that industry already exercises daily without naming precisely.

</details>


### [196] [From Minds to Models: The Intersection of Psychology and LLM Behaviours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27579)
*Oliver Guidetti,Reza Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用内隐联想测验的提示词改编版本，探讨ChatGPT在开放式文本中是否产生跨种族条件的情感差异。126个提示词提交到三个GPT模型产生378个响应，双因素方差分析发现种族条件的微弱主效应（p=.042），但在秩变换敏感性分析和Tukey校正后均不显著，证据薄弱且依赖于分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的决策过程复杂、非线性且难以解释，类似于人类心智。研究者希望利用心理学研究不可观察心理过程的方法来检验LLM行为，特别是在政府和医疗等高风险领域的应用背景下，评估模型是否存在隐含的种族偏见。

Method: 基于内隐联想测验的提示词改编，将14个基础问题与8个种族类别及1个种族无关对照组交叉，共生成126个提示词。每个提示词分别提交至GPT-3.5T、GPT-4和GPT-4T，共获得378个响应。情感得分从分类标签和源得分推导（正面标签保留源得分，负面标签取负值，中性编码为零），采用双因素方差分析及秩变换敏感性分析。

Result: 双因素方差分析发现种族条件的小主效应（F(8,351)=2.04, p=.042, partial-eta squared=.044），但模型间无显著差异，也无交互作用。秩变换敏感性分析未保留效应（p=.145），Tukey校正后无显著配对差异。未校正的欧洲-原住民澳大利亚比较虽显著，但为事后选择的结果，仅作假设生成用途。

Conclusion: 证据表明情感差异微弱且依赖分析方法。情感评分无法区分评价性偏见与提示词所引发的历史内容的情感效价。作者呼吁进行跨学科合作，开发测量模型偏见的可行为化指标，并提出解决这些局限性的设计修改方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compared with the human mind because their decision-making is complex, non-linear and difficult to interpret. Psychological methods developed to investigate unobservable mental processes may therefore help examine LLM behaviour, particularly in government and healthcare. Building on prompt-based adaptations of the Implicit Association Test, this study tested whether ChatGPT produced sentiment differences across racial conditions in open-ended text. Fourteen base questions were crossed with eight racial categories and a race-agnostic control, producing 126 prompts. Each was submitted once to GPT-3.5T, GPT-4 and GPT-4T, yielding 378 responses. Sentiment scores were derived from categorical labels and source scores: positive labels retained the source score, negative labels were assigned its negative, and neutral responses were coded zero. A two-way ANOVA found a small main effect of racial condition, F(8, 351) = 2.04, p = .042, partial-eta squared = .044, but no effect of model, F(2, 351) = 0.07, p = .933, and no interaction, F(16, 351) = 0.23, p = .999. However, the effect was not retained in a rank-transformed sensitivity analysis, F(8, 351) = 1.53, p = .145, and Tukey-corrected comparisons found no significant pairwise differences. An uncorrected European-Indigenous Australian comparison was significant, but was selected post hoc and is reported only as hypothesis-generating. Evidence for sentiment differences was therefore weak and analysis-dependent. Sentiment scoring also cannot distinguish evaluative bias from the valence of historical content elicited by a prompt. We outline design changes needed to address these limitations and argue for interdisciplinary development of behavioural measures of model bias.
  Keywords: Implicit Bias, Psychological Research Methods, Artificial Intelligence, ChatGPT, Large Language Models, Sentiment Analysis

</details>


### [197] [Wiring diagram extraction and gluing: a case study in classifying figure skating jumps using 3D dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27598)
*Jason Lo,Mohammadnima Jafari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"胶合接线图"（gluing wiring diagrams）理论，使得Hasse聚类算法可以通过迭代方式应用，从而克服其随聚类数量增长而产生的组合爆炸问题，并在花样滑冰跳跃视频分类任务中验证了该理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hasse聚类算法能够从序列数据中提取公共模式并以图形形式表示，但随着预期聚类数量的增长，算法的组合复杂度会导致计算不可行。因此需要一种方法使算法能够分解为可迭代执行的子问题。

Method: 提出了一种胶合接线图的理论框架，使得多次迭代应用Hasse聚类能够达到与单次应用相同的结果，从而将大规模聚类问题分解为可管理的小规模子问题。

Result: 在花样滑冰跳跃视频分类的实验中验证了该理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 胶合接线图理论为Hasse聚类的可扩展性提供了理论保障，使算法能够应对大规模聚类问题而不损失结果的一致性。

Abstract: Hasse clustering is an algorithm that extracts common patterns in sequential data and represents them in graphical forms. As the number of expected clusters grows, however, the algorithm can become infeasible to run due to combinatorial complexity. In this article, we describe a theory of gluing wiring diagrams, allowing iterative applications of Hasse clustering to achieve the same result as a single application. We test our theory in the context of classifying videos of figure skating jumps.

</details>


### [198] [World Action Planner: Generalizable Decision-Making with Action-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27599)
*Xiangcheng Zhang,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI
